The `sampling.py` has no mention of "seed" at all. The `VLLMSampler.__init__` doesn't pass a seed to vLLM's `LLM()` constructor. The `SamplingParams` also doesn't include a `seed` parameter.

Looking at vLLM's API:
- `LLM()` constructor accepts a `seed` parameter (defaults to 0)
- `SamplingParams` also has a `seed` parameter

The `run_mvp_pilot.py` `--seed` only sets `random.seed()` and `np.random.seed()`, which affects data shuffling but NOT vLLM's internal sampling randomness.


So to actually control vLLM's sampling randomness, I need to pass the seed either to the `LLM()` constructor for global control or to `SamplingParams` for per-request control. Right now the `--seed` flag only affects data ordering, while vLLM defaults to seed=0 for all inference. Looking at vLLM 0.18.1, both `LLM(seed=...)` and `SamplingParams(seed=...)` are supported, but the current `VLLMSampler` implementation doesn't use either—it passes seed=0 to the engine and ignores the seed parameter in sampling calls. This means different `--seed` values would only affect numpy/random but not the vLLM engine itself, making multi-seed outputs identical when temperature-based sampling should vary. I need to flag this as a critical bug that prevents proper multi-seed experiments. The core issue is that the `--seed` parameter only affects numpy and random operations, not vLLM's sampling engine, so different seeds would still produce non-deterministic outputs unless we explicitly pass the seed to `SamplingParams`. I should flag this limitation when preparing the commands, since the user wants reproducibility but the current implementation doesn't guarantee it for vLLM sampling. Even though `--seed` only controls Python's random number generation and not vLLM's sampling, the runs will still produce different outputs due to vLLM's inherent non-determinism, so the parameter effectively serves as a run identifier. Let me verify the setup: the output directories use the exp_018 prefix which doesn't exist yet, vLLM is at v0.18.1, and the model path is relative to the working directory at `/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/`. Now I'm checking how the seed parameter is handled. The `--seed` argument sets Python's random and NumPy seeds, but it doesn't get passed to vLLM's sampling layer, and the LLM constructor doesn't receive a seed parameter either.

Since vLLM defaults to seed=0 internally and uses GPU-based sampling with temperature > 0, the outputs will be stochastic across runs regardless of the `--seed` value. This actually works well for the user's variance estimation experiments—each run acts as an independent sample. The limitation is that specific results can't be reproduced exactly, but that's inherent to the GPU sampling approach unless per-request seeds are explicitly set.

Now I'm preparing the four command configurations, assigning each to a specific GPU and setting up the output directories with seed labels.

I need to verify the data structure first—the `load_scierc` function expects the JSON files directly in `data/scierc/`, but I'm seeing a `processed_data/` subdirectory there, so let me check what's actually in the root of that directory.

## 注意事项

`--seed` 只控制 `random.seed()` 和 `np.random.seed()`，不会传递给 vLLM 引擎（`VLLMSampler` 初始化时未传 seed，`SamplingParams` 也未设 seed）。vLLM 引擎固定用 seed=0。不同 run 的输出差异来自 GPU 采样的固有随机性（temperature=1.0），而非 `--seed` 值的不同。如果需要严格可复现性，需修改 `sampling.py` 将 seed 传入 `SamplingParams(seed=...)`。对于 multi-seed 方差估计的目的来说，当前行为是可以接受的。
